11:T51f,;(function () {
  try {
    var t = localStorage.getItem('theme')
    if (t === 'light' || t === 'dark') {
      document.documentElement.classList.add(t)
      document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', t)
    } else if (t === 'system' && typeof window.matchMedia === 'function') {
      var v = window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches ? 'dark' : 'light'
      document.documentElement.classList.add(v)
      document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', v)
    } else {
      document.documentElement.classList.add('dark')
      document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', 'dark')
    }
  } catch (e) {}
  try {
    if (localStorage.getItem('sidebar-collapsed') === 'true') {
      document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-sidebar-collapsed', 'true')
    }
  } catch (e) {}
  // Reading mode: apply before paint so there is no flash even on full-page reloads.
  // The background inline style mirrors what ReadingModeContext sets via JS.
  // We read the CSS variable directly because the theme class is already on <html>.
  try {
    if (localStorage.getItem('reading-mode') === 'true') {
      document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-reading-mode', 'true')
      document.documentElement.style.background = 'hsl(var(--background))'
    }
  } catch (e) {}

200:["$","p","p-313",{"className":"$undefined","data-indent":"2em","children":"方法：可分离的 1D de Casteljau 算法"}]
201:["$","blockquote","blockquote-2",{"children":["\n",["$","p","p-0",{"className":"$undefined","data-indent":"2em","children":"Separable 1D de Casteljau 算法是 de Casteljau 算法的一种变体，用于在一维空间中对 Bézier 曲线进行求值。它是通过将原始的 de Casteljau 算法进行简化和优化而得到的。"}],"\n",["$","p","p-1",{"className":"$undefined","data-indent":"2em","children":"de Casteljau 算法是一种递归方法，用于在二维空间中计算 Bézier 曲线上的点。然而，当 Bézier 曲线是一维的（即线性曲线）时，可以使用更简单的算法来进行计算，这就是 Separable 1D de Casteljau 算法。"}],"\n",["$","p","p-2",{"className":"$undefined","data-indent":"2em","children":"这个算法的关键思想是将一维 Bézier 曲线的控制点集合视为一系列水平或垂直的线段，并通过对这些线段进行递归求解来计算曲线上的点。由于每个维度都可以单独处理，因此称为\"可分离\"。"}],"\n",["$","p","p-3",{"className":"$undefined","children":"算法的步骤如下："}],"\n",["$","ol","ol-0",{"children":["\n",["$","li","li-0",{"children":"对于给定的参数值 t，在每个维度上对控制点进行插值。例如，在水平方向上进行插值，然后在垂直方向上进行插值，或者反之。"}],"\n",["$","li","li-1",{"children":"重复此过程，直到每个维度上只剩下一个点为止。"}],"\n",["$","li","li-2",{"children":"最终得到的点就是 Bézier 曲线上的点。"}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","p","p-4",{"className":"$undefined","data-indent":"2em","children":"Separable 1D de Casteljau 算法相对于传统的 de Casteljau 算法来说更加简单和高效，特别适用于一维 Bézier 曲线的求值。"}],"\n"]}]
